1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	
4	cd /user_4813494d/openbmb
5	
6	# Ensure the project venv is active (scripts must not depend on caller's shell).
7	SALA_VENV="/opt/SGLang-MiniCPM-SALA/sglang_minicpm_sala_env"
8	if [[ "${VIRTUAL_ENV:-}" != "${SALA_VENV}" || "$(command -v python3)" != "${SALA_VENV}/bin/python3" ]]; then
9	    # shellcheck disable=SC1091
10	    source "${SALA_VENV}/bin/activate"
11	fi
12	
13	# EAGLE-3 speculative decoding server (current production config)
14	# - spec_steps=2, topk=2, dtn=5  (chain verify)
15	# - target: NVFP4 (GPTQ + FourOverSix), hybrid Marlin/CUTLASS decode @ threshold=48
16	# - draft:  v3 FP4_QAT, pure Marlin W4A16, rope_theta=1M
17	# - MARS:   D5 theta=0.85, D7 theta=0.5
18	#
19	# Env overrides:
20	#   EAGLE_SPEC_STEPS, EAGLE_TOPK, EAGLE_TARGET_MODEL, EAGLE_DRAFT_MODEL
21	#   SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE=0 to disable long-prompt prefill plan reuse
22	#   SGLANG_MINICPM_FILL_COMPRESS_BUFFERS=1 to restore old decode replay fills
23	#   SGLANG_SIMPLE_GLA_DIRECT_DECODE=0 to disable the direct-state decode kernel
24	
25	SPEC_STEPS="${EAGLE_SPEC_STEPS:-3}"
26	TOPK="${EAGLE_TOPK:-2}"
27	# dtn = 1 + topk * spec_steps (tree nodes)
28	DTN=$((1 + TOPK * SPEC_STEPS))
29	TARGET_MODEL="${EAGLE_TARGET_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4-det}"
30	DRAFT_MODEL="${EAGLE_DRAFT_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/models/v2mix_20k_s3500_ood757}"
31	MARS_THETA="${EAGLE_MARS_THETA:-1}"
32	D5_MARS_THETA="${EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA:-0.75}"
33	D7_MARS_THETA="${EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA:-0.5}"
34	RANDOM_SEED_ARGS=()
35	if [[ -n "${SGLANG_RANDOM_SEED:-}" ]]; then
36	    RANDOM_SEED_ARGS=(--random-seed "${SGLANG_RANDOM_SEED}")
37	fi
38	
39	echo "[start_eagle] MARS global=${MARS_THETA} D5=${D5_MARS_THETA} D7=${D7_MARS_THETA}"
40	
41	EAGLE_FORCE_NO_ACCEPT="${EAGLE_FORCE_NO_ACCEPT:-0}" \
42	EAGLE_TRACE_FILE="${EAGLE_TRACE_FILE:-}" \
43	SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE="${SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE:-1}" \
44	SGLANG_MINICPM_FILL_COMPRESS_BUFFERS="${SGLANG_MINICPM_FILL_COMPRESS_BUFFERS:-0}" \
45	SGLANG_SIMPLE_GLA_DIRECT_DECODE="${SGLANG_SIMPLE_GLA_DIRECT_DECODE:-1}" \
46	SGLANG_SIMPLE_GLA_DECODE_WARPS="${SGLANG_SIMPLE_GLA_DECODE_WARPS:-4}" \
47	SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE="${SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE:-/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json}" \
48	SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD="${SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD:-48}" \
49	SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE_OOP="${SGLANG_MINICPM_FUSED_GLA_QK_NORM_ROPE_OOP:-1}" \
50	SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_RMSNORM_SIGMOID_MUL="${SGLANG_MINICPM_GLA_FUSED_RMSNORM_SIGMOID_MUL:-1}" \
51	SGLANG_ENABLE_SPEC_V2=0 \
52	EAGLE_MARS_THETA="${MARS_THETA}" \
53	EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA="${D5_MARS_THETA}" \
54	EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA="${D7_MARS_THETA}" \
55	EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE="${EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE:-1}" \
56	EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS="${EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS:-31}" \
57	EAGLE_NO_SPEC_LEAVE_BS="${EAGLE_NO_SPEC_LEAVE_BS:-28}" \
58	EAGLE_D7_BS="${EAGLE_D7_BS:-1}" \
59	EAGLE_D7_LEAVE_BS="${EAGLE_D7_LEAVE_BS:-3}" \
60	EAGLE_D5_TOPK="${EAGLE_D5_TOPK:-2}" \
61	EAGLE_D5_STEPS="${EAGLE_D5_STEPS:-3}" \
62	EAGLE_D5_DTN="${EAGLE_D5_DTN:-7}" \
63	EAGLE_D7_TOPK="${EAGLE_D7_TOPK:-2}" \
64	EAGLE_D7_STEPS="${EAGLE_D7_STEPS:-5}" \
65	EAGLE_D7_DTN="${EAGLE_D7_DTN:-11}" \
66	python3 -m sglang.launch_server \
67	    --model-path "${TARGET_MODEL}" \
68	    --trust-remote-code --port 30000 \
69	    --mem-fraction-static 0.75 \
70	    --max-running-requests 36 \
71	    --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer \
72	    --chunked-prefill-size 8192 --disable-radix-cache \
73	    --skip-server-warmup \
74	    --dense-as-sparse \
75	    --quantization modelopt_fp4 \

80	    --speculative-draft-attention-backend flashinfer \

82	    "${RANDOM_SEED_ARGS[@]}"

1	"""Dynamic speculative-decoding mode selection for MiniCPM-SALA.
2	
3	Picks one of three modes per decode step based on the running batch size:
4	
5	    NO_SPEC      : bs >= EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS         (target_worker direct decode)
6	    MARS_D5      : default spec config            (chain verify, dtn=5 by default)
7	    MARS_D7      : bs <= EAGLE_D7_BS              (chain verify, dtn=7 by default)
8	
9	Hysteresis (separate enter/leave thresholds) prevents prefill bursts from
10	flipping NO_SPEC mid-bench. Bench traffic is monotonically decreasing on bs,
11	so transitions are essentially one-way: NO_SPEC -> MARS_D5 -> MARS_D7.
12	
13	All thresholds and per-mode spec parameters are env-driven so we can sweep
14	without rebuilding. Enable with EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE=1.
15	"""
16	
17	import enum
18	import logging
19	import os
20	from dataclasses import dataclass
21	
22	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
23	
24	
25	class SpecMode(enum.Enum):
26	    NO_SPEC = "no_spec"
27	    MARS_D5 = "mars_d5"
28	    MARS_D7 = "mars_d7"
29	
30	
31	@dataclass(frozen=True)
32	class SpecModeConfig:
33	    enabled: bool
34	    enter_no_spec_bs: int
35	    leave_no_spec_bs: int
36	    enter_d7_bs: int
37	    leave_d7_bs: int
38	    d5_topk: int
39	    d5_steps: int
40	    d5_dtn: int
41	    d7_topk: int
42	    d7_steps: int
43	    d7_dtn: int
44	    mars_theta: float          # global fallback (legacy EAGLE_MARS_THETA)
45	    d5_mars_theta: float       # per-mode override; -1 means "use mars_theta"
46	    d7_mars_theta: float
47	
48	    def effective_d5_theta(self) -> float:
49	        return self.d5_mars_theta if self.d5_mars_theta >= 0.0 else self.mars_theta
50	
51	    def effective_d7_theta(self) -> float:
52	        return self.d7_mars_theta if self.d7_mars_theta >= 0.0 else self.mars_theta
53	
54	    def summary(self) -> str:
55	        if not self.enabled:
56	            return "EAGLE dynamic mode: DISABLED (static D5)"
57	        return (
58	            "EAGLE dynamic mode: ENABLED | "
59	            f"NO_SPEC bs>={self.enter_no_spec_bs} (leave<={self.leave_no_spec_bs}) | "
60	            f"D7 bs<={self.enter_d7_bs} (leave>={self.leave_d7_bs}) | "
61	            f"D5 (topk={self.d5_topk}, steps={self.d5_steps}, dtn={self.d5_dtn}, "
62	            f"theta={self.effective_d5_theta()}) | "
63	            f"D7 (topk={self.d7_topk}, steps={self.d7_steps}, dtn={self.d7_dtn}, "
64	            f"theta={self.effective_d7_theta()})"
65	        )
66	
67	
68	def _env_int(name: str, default: int) -> int:
69	    raw = os.environ.get(name)
70	    if raw is None or raw == "":
71	        return default
72	    try:
73	        return int(raw)
74	    except ValueError:
75	        logger.warning("[spec-mode] %s=%r not int, fallback %d", name, raw, default)
76	        return default
77	
78	
79	def _env_float(name: str, default: float) -> float:
80	    raw = os.environ.get(name)
81	    if raw is None or raw == "":
82	        return default
83	    try:
84	        return float(raw)
85	    except ValueError:
86	        logger.warning("[spec-mode] %s=%r not float, fallback %f", name, raw, default)
87	        return default
88	
89	
90	def load_config() -> SpecModeConfig:
91	    cfg = SpecModeConfig(
92	        enabled=os.environ.get("EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE", "0") == "1",
93	        enter_no_spec_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_NO_SPEC_BS", 31),
94	        leave_no_spec_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_NO_SPEC_LEAVE_BS", 28),
95	        enter_d7_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_BS", 1),
96	        leave_d7_bs=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_LEAVE_BS", 3),
97	        d5_topk=_env_int("EAGLE_D5_TOPK", 2),
98	        d5_steps=_env_int("EAGLE_D5_STEPS", 2),
99	        d5_dtn=_env_int("EAGLE_D5_DTN", 5),
100	        d7_topk=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_TOPK", 2),
101	        d7_steps=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_STEPS", 3),
102	        d7_dtn=_env_int("EAGLE_D7_DTN", 7),
103	        mars_theta=_env_float("EAGLE_MARS_THETA", -1.0),
104	        # Per-mode MARS theta overrides. -1 = inherit global mars_theta.
105	        # S1 (bs=1) runs entirely in D7, so a more aggressive D7 theta can
106	        # boost S1 accept-length without affecting D5/no-spec quality.
107	        d5_mars_theta=_env_float("EAGLE_D5_MARS_THETA", -1.0),
108	        d7_mars_theta=_env_float("EAGLE_D7_MARS_THETA", -1.0),
109	    )
110	    _validate(cfg)
111	    return cfg
112	
113	
114	def _validate(cfg: SpecModeConfig) -> None:
115	    if not cfg.enabled:
116	        return
117	    if cfg.leave_no_spec_bs >= cfg.enter_no_spec_bs:
118	        logger.warning(
119	            "[spec-mode] leave_no_spec_bs(%d) >= enter_no_spec_bs(%d) disables hysteresis",
120	            cfg.leave_no_spec_bs, cfg.enter_no_spec_bs,
121	        )
122	    if cfg.leave_d7_bs <= cfg.enter_d7_bs:
123	        logger.warning(
124	            "[spec-mode] leave_d7_bs(%d) <= enter_d7_bs(%d) disables hysteresis",
125	            cfg.leave_d7_bs, cfg.enter_d7_bs,
126	        )
127	    for tag, topk, steps, dtn in (
128	        ("D5", cfg.d5_topk, cfg.d5_steps, cfg.d5_dtn),
129	        ("D7", cfg.d7_topk, cfg.d7_steps, cfg.d7_dtn),
130	    ):
131	        expected = 1 + topk * steps
132	        if dtn != expected:
133	            logger.warning(
134	                "[spec-mode] %s dtn=%d != 1+topk*steps=%d (chain verify expects equality)",
135	                tag, dtn, expected,
136	            )
137	
138	
139	class SpecModeSelector:
140	    """Pick spec mode for a decode batch based on running BS.
141	
142	    Hysteresis guards against prefill bursts re-entering NO_SPEC mid-bench;
143	    under monotonically-decreasing bs traffic, the path is effectively
144	    NO_SPEC -> MARS_D5 -> MARS_D7 with no backtracking.
145	    """
146	
147	    def __init__(self, config: SpecModeConfig):
148	        self.config = config
149	        self.current = SpecMode.MARS_D5  # initial default; first decode step overrides
150	
151	    def select(self, bs: int) -> SpecMode:
152	        cfg = self.config
153	        if not cfg.enabled:
154	            return SpecMode.MARS_D5
155	
156	        prev = self.current
157	        if prev == SpecMode.NO_SPEC:
158	            if bs <= cfg.leave_no_spec_bs:
159	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D7 if bs <= cfg.enter_d7_bs else SpecMode.MARS_D5
160	            else:
161	                new = SpecMode.NO_SPEC
162	        elif prev == SpecMode.MARS_D7:
163	            if bs >= cfg.enter_no_spec_bs:
164	                new = SpecMode.NO_SPEC
165	            elif bs >= cfg.leave_d7_bs:
166	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D5
167	            else:
168	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D7
169	        else:  # MARS_D5
170	            if bs >= cfg.enter_no_spec_bs:
171	                new = SpecMode.NO_SPEC
172	            elif bs <= cfg.enter_d7_bs:
173	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D7
174	            else:
175	                new = SpecMode.MARS_D5
176	
177	        if new != prev:
178	            logger.info("[spec-mode] %s -> %s (bs=%d)", prev.value, new.value, bs)
179	        self.current = new
180	        return new
181	
182	
183	def selector_from_env() -> SpecModeSelector:
184	    cfg = load_config()
185	    logger.info("[spec-mode] %s", cfg.summary())
186	    return SpecModeSelector(cfg)
187

274	def evict_from_tree_cache(tree_cache: BasePrefixCache | None, num_tokens: int):
275	    if tree_cache is None:
276	        return
277	
278	    if isinstance(tree_cache, (SWAChunkCache, ChunkCache)):
279	        return
280	
281	    allocator = tree_cache.token_to_kv_pool_allocator
282	
283	    # Check if this is a hybrid allocator
284	    if hasattr(allocator, "full_available_size"):
285	        # Hybrid allocator
286	        full_available_size = allocator.full_available_size()
287	        swa_available_size = allocator.swa_available_size()
288	
289	        if full_available_size < num_tokens or swa_available_size < num_tokens:
290	            full_num_tokens = max(0, num_tokens - full_available_size)
291	            swa_num_tokens = max(0, num_tokens - swa_available_size)
292	            tree_cache.evict(full_num_tokens, swa_num_tokens)
293	    else:
294	        # Standard allocator
295	        if allocator.available_size() < num_tokens:
296	            tree_cache.evict(num_tokens)
297

299	def alloc_paged_token_slots_extend(
300	    tree_cache: BasePrefixCache,
301	    prefix_lens: torch.Tensor,
302	    prefix_lens_cpu: torch.Tensor,
303	    seq_lens: torch.Tensor,
304	    seq_lens_cpu: torch.Tensor,
305	    last_loc: torch.Tensor,
306	    extend_num_tokens: int,
307	    backup_state: bool = False,
308	):
309	    # Over estimate the number of tokens: assume each request needs a new page.
310	    allocator = tree_cache.token_to_kv_pool_allocator
311	    num_tokens = extend_num_tokens + len(seq_lens_cpu) * allocator.page_size
312	    evict_from_tree_cache(tree_cache, num_tokens)
313	
314	    state = None
315	    if backup_state:
316	        state = allocator.backup_state()
317	
318	    out_cache_loc = allocator.alloc_extend(
319	        prefix_lens,
320	        prefix_lens_cpu,
321	        seq_lens,
322	        seq_lens_cpu,
323	        last_loc,
324	        extend_num_tokens,
325	    )
326	
327	    if out_cache_loc is None:
328	        error_msg = (
329	            f"Prefill out of memory. Try to lower your batch size.\n"
330	            f"Try to allocate {extend_num_tokens} tokens.\n"
331	            f"{available_and_evictable_str(tree_cache)}"
332	        )
333	        logger.error(error_msg)
334	        if tree_cache is not None:
335	            tree_cache.pretty_print()
336	        raise RuntimeError(error_msg)
337	
338	    return (out_cache_loc, state) if backup_state else out_cache_loc
339

341	def alloc_req_slots(
342	    req_to_token_pool: ReqToTokenPool,
343	    num_reqs: int,
344	    reqs: list[Req] | None,
345	    tree_cache: BasePrefixCache | None,
346	) -> list[int]:
347	    """Allocate request slots from the pool."""
348	    if isinstance(req_to_token_pool, HybridReqToTokenPool):
349	        mamba_available_size = req_to_token_pool.mamba_pool.available_size()
350	        factor = (
351	            MAMBA_STATE_PER_REQ_PREFIX_CACHE
352	            if isinstance(tree_cache, MambaRadixCache)
353	            else MAMBA_STATE_PER_REQ_NO_CACHE
354	        )
355	        mamba_state_needed = num_reqs * factor
356	        if mamba_available_size < mamba_state_needed:
357	            if tree_cache is not None and isinstance(tree_cache, MambaRadixCache):
358	                mamba_num = max(0, mamba_state_needed - mamba_available_size)
359	                tree_cache.evict_mamba(mamba_num)
360	        req_pool_indices = req_to_token_pool.alloc(num_reqs, reqs)
361	    else:
362	        req_pool_indices = req_to_token_pool.alloc(num_reqs)
363	
364	    if req_pool_indices is None:
365	        raise RuntimeError(
366	            "alloc_req_slots runs out of memory. "
367	            "Please set a smaller number for `--max-running-requests`. "
368	            f"{req_to_token_pool.available_size()=}, "
369	            f"{num_reqs=}, "
370	        )
371	    return req_pool_indices
372	
373	
374	def alloc_for_extend(
375	    batch: ScheduleBatch,
376	) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, list[int]]:
377	    """
378	    Allocate KV cache for extend batch and write to req_to_token_pool.
379	
380	    Returns:
381	        out_cache_loc: allocated cache locations
382	        req_pool_indices_device: request pool indices at a device tensor
383	        req_pool_indices: request pool indices as list
384	    """
385	    # free out-of-window swa tokens
386	    if isinstance(batch.tree_cache, SWAChunkCache):
387	        for req, pre_len in zip(batch.reqs, batch.prefix_lens):
388	            if batch.enable_overlap:
389	                # In chunked prefill case, when the second extend batch is scheduling, the first extend batch is still running, so we cannot evict swa tokens
390	                if req.extend_batch_idx < 2:
391	                    continue
392	                else:
393	                    batch.tree_cache.evict_swa(
394	                        req, pre_len - batch.tree_cache.chunked_prefill_size
395	                    )
396	            else:
397	                batch.tree_cache.evict_swa(req, pre_len)
398	
399	    bs = len(batch.reqs)

319	class PrefillAdder:
320	    def __init__(
321	        self,
322	        page_size: int,
323	        tree_cache: BasePrefixCache,
324	        token_to_kv_pool_allocator: BaseTokenToKVPoolAllocator,
325	        running_batch: ScheduleBatch,
326	        new_token_ratio: float,
327	        rem_input_tokens: int,
328	        rem_chunk_tokens: Optional[int],
329	        mixed_with_decode_tokens: int = 0,
330	        priority_scheduling_preemption_threshold: int = 0,
331	        prefill_max_requests: Optional[int] = None,
332	        prefill_delayer_single_pass: Optional[PrefillDelayerSinglePassExecutor] = None,
333	    ):
334	        self.page_size = page_size
335	        self.tree_cache = tree_cache
336	        self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator = token_to_kv_pool_allocator
337	        self.running_batch = running_batch
338	        self.new_token_ratio = new_token_ratio
339	        self.rem_input_tokens = rem_input_tokens - mixed_with_decode_tokens
340	        self.rem_chunk_tokens = rem_chunk_tokens
341	        if self.rem_chunk_tokens is not None:
342	            self.rem_chunk_tokens -= mixed_with_decode_tokens
343	        self.rem_total_token_offset = mixed_with_decode_tokens
344	        self.cur_rem_token_offset = mixed_with_decode_tokens
345	
346	        self.req_states = None
347	        self.can_run_list = []
348	        self.preempt_list = []
349	        self.new_chunked_req = None
350	        self.log_hit_tokens = 0
351	        # TODO(lsyin): report the real input tokens excluding page alignment
352	        self.log_input_tokens = 0
353	
354	        if running_batch is not None:
355	            self.rem_total_token_offset += sum(
356	                [
357	                    self._get_running_request_total_token_offset(r)
358	                    for r in running_batch.reqs
359	                ]
360	            )
361	
362	        self.is_hybrid_swa = isinstance(
363	            self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator, SWATokenToKVPoolAllocator
364	        )
365	        self.is_hybrid_ssm_cache = isinstance(self.tree_cache, MambaRadixCache)
366	
367	        self.priority_scheduling_preemption_threshold = (
368	            priority_scheduling_preemption_threshold
369	        )
370	        self.nsa_prefill_cp_in_seq_split = is_nsa_prefill_cp_in_seq_split()
371	        self.prefill_max_requests = prefill_max_requests
372	        self.prefill_delayer_single_pass = prefill_delayer_single_pass

374	    def _get_running_request_total_token_offset(self, req: Req) -> int:
375	        return (
376	            min(
377	                (req.sampling_params.max_new_tokens - len(req.output_ids)),
378	                CLIP_MAX_NEW_TOKENS,
379	            )
380	            * self.new_token_ratio
381	        )

385	        if self.is_hybrid_swa:
386	            available_and_evictable = min(
387	                self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.full_available_size()
388	                + self.tree_cache.full_evictable_size(),
389	                self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.swa_available_size()
390	                + self.tree_cache.swa_evictable_size(),
391	            )
392	        elif self.is_hybrid_ssm_cache:
393	            available_and_evictable = (

395	                + self.tree_cache.full_evictable_size()
396	            )
397	        else:
398	            available_and_evictable = (
399	                self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.available_size()
400	                + self.tree_cache.evictable_size()
401	            )
402	        return available_and_evictable - self.rem_total_token_offset
403	
404	    @property
405	    def cur_rem_tokens(self):
406	        if self.is_hybrid_swa:
407	            available_and_evictable = min(

409	                + self.tree_cache.full_evictable_size(),
410	                self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.swa_available_size()
411	                + self.tree_cache.swa_evictable_size(),
412	            )
413	        elif self.is_hybrid_ssm_cache:

416	                + self.tree_cache.full_evictable_size()
417	            )
418	        else:

426	    def ceil_paged_tokens(self, tokens: int) -> int:
427	        return -(-tokens // self.page_size) * self.page_size

429	    def budget_state(self):
430	        if self.rem_total_tokens <= 0 or self.cur_rem_tokens <= 0:
431	            return AddReqResult.NO_TOKEN
432	
433	        if self.rem_input_tokens <= 0 or (
434	            self.rem_chunk_tokens is not None and self.rem_chunk_tokens <= 0
435	        ):
436	            return AddReqResult.OTHER
437	
438	        return AddReqResult.CONTINUE
439	
440	    def _update_prefill_budget(
441	        self, prefix_len: int, extend_input_len: int, max_new_tokens: int
442	    ):
443	        # TODO(lsyin): check this workaround logic, which only ensures the prefill will not out of memory, and may be too conservative
444	        extend_input_len = self.ceil_paged_tokens(extend_input_len)
445	
446	        self.rem_total_token_offset += extend_input_len + max_new_tokens
447	        self.cur_rem_token_offset += extend_input_len
448	        self.rem_input_tokens -= extend_input_len
449	        if self.rem_chunk_tokens is not None:
450	            self.rem_chunk_tokens -= extend_input_len
451	
452	        self.log_hit_tokens += prefix_len
453	        self.log_input_tokens += extend_input_len
454	
455	    def add_chunked_req(self, req: Req):
456	        _rem_tokens = min(self.rem_chunk_tokens, int(self.rem_total_tokens))
457	        # The chunked_req must be added to the list; otherwise, it will cause a memory leak.
458	        # Therefore, in certain cases where _rem_tokens <= 0, it should be replaced with rem_chunk_tokens.
459	        if _rem_tokens <= 0:
460	            _rem_tokens = self.rem_chunk_tokens
461	        truncated = req.extend_input_len > _rem_tokens
462	        req.set_extend_input_len(min(req.extend_input_len, _rem_tokens))
463	        req.fill_ids = req.fill_ids[: len(req.prefix_indices) + req.extend_input_len]
464	        self.can_run_list.append(req)
465	        self._update_prefill_budget(
466	            0,
467	            req.extend_input_len,
468	            (
469	                min(req.sampling_params.max_new_tokens, CLIP_MAX_NEW_TOKENS)
470	                if not truncated
471	                else 0
472	            ),
473	        )
474	
475	        # Return if chunked prefill not finished
476	        return req if truncated else None
477	
478	    @contextmanager
479	    def _lock_node(self, last_node: TreeNode):
480	        try:
481	            ret = self.tree_cache.inc_lock_ref(last_node)
482	            yield None
483	        finally:
484	            if isinstance(self.tree_cache, SWARadixCache):
485	                self.tree_cache.dec_lock_ref(last_node, ret)
486	            else:
487	                self.tree_cache.dec_lock_ref(last_node)
488	
489	    def add_one_req_ignore_eos(self, req: Req):
490	        # Early exit if no enough tokens for the input tokens
491	        if self.ceil_paged_tokens(req.extend_input_len) > min(
492	            self.cur_rem_tokens, self.rem_total_tokens
493	        ):
494	            return AddReqResult.NO_TOKEN
495	
496	        def add_req_state(r, insert_sort=False):
497	            new_token_ratio = (
498	                1.0 if r.sampling_params.ignore_eos else self.new_token_ratio
499	            )

546	    def init_model_worker(self):
547	        # When dynamic spec mode is enabled, the target/draft cuda graphs must
548	        # be captured against the *largest* dtn so we can switch dtn at runtime
549	        # (D5 dtn=5 <-> D7 dtn=7) without overflowing pre-allocated input buffers.
550	        # The target graph reads `server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens`
551	        # directly during capture (cuda_graph_runner.py:287-290), so we bump
552	        # it before capture and restore to the CLI value afterwards. Restoring
553	        # is critical: scheduler budget — `decode_mem_cache_buf_multiplier`
554	        # in __init__ — multiplies prefill cache reservation by dtn+topk*steps,
555	        # and a max-bumped dtn shrinks usable KV pool enough to make
556	        # _get_new_batch_prefill_raw schedule a chunk that prepare_for_extend
557	        # cannot then alloc → hard OOM crash (not retract). cuda_graph_runner
558	        # already cached `num_tokens_per_bs` from the bumped value during
559	        # capture, so the runtime graph dispatch is unaffected by the restore.
560	        bumped_dtn = bumped_steps = None
561	        original_dtn = original_steps = None
562	        if self.spec_algorithm.is_eagle():
563	            from sglang.srt.speculative.spec_mode import load_config as _load_spec_cfg
564	            spec_cfg = _load_spec_cfg()
565	            if spec_cfg.enabled:
566	                max_dtn = max(spec_cfg.d5_dtn, spec_cfg.d7_dtn)
567	                original_dtn = self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens
568	                if original_dtn < max_dtn:
569	                    logger.info(
570	                        "[spec-mode] bumping server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens "
571	                        "%d -> %d for cuda graph capture; will restore",
572	                        original_dtn, max_dtn,
573	                    )
574	                    self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens = max_dtn
575	                    bumped_dtn = max_dtn
576	                # EAGLEDraftExtendCudaGraphRunner sizes buffers from
577	                # server_args.speculative_num_steps + 1; bump it for capture,
578	                # restore for budget logic.
579	                max_steps = max(spec_cfg.d5_steps, spec_cfg.d7_steps)
580	                original_steps = self.server_args.speculative_num_steps
581	                if original_steps < max_steps:
582	                    logger.info(
583	                        "[spec-mode] bumping server_args.speculative_num_steps "
584	                        "%d -> %d for cuda graph capture; will restore",
585	                        original_steps, max_steps,
586	                    )
587	                    self.server_args.speculative_num_steps = max_steps
588	                    bumped_steps = max_steps
589	        self.init_tp_model_worker()
590	        self.init_draft_worker()
591	
592	        # Restore CLI values now that cuda graphs are captured. Scheduler
593	        # budget / decode_mem_cache_buf_multiplier (computed downstream) reads
594	        # these and must reflect the steady-state spec-decode footprint, not
595	        # the inflated max needed only for capture.
596	        if bumped_dtn is not None:
597	            self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens = original_dtn
598	            logger.info(
599	                "[spec-mode] restored speculative_num_draft_tokens %d -> %d "

604	            self.server_args.speculative_num_steps = original_steps
605	            logger.info(

607	                "(post-capture)",
608	                bumped_steps, original_steps,
609	            )

1789	    def new_page_count_next_decode(self, selected_indices: Optional[List[int]] = None):
1790	        page_size = self.token_to_kv_pool_allocator.page_size
1791	        requests = (
1792	            self.reqs
1793	            if selected_indices is None
1794	            else [self.reqs[i] for i in selected_indices]
1795	        )
1796	        if page_size == 1:
1797	            return len(requests)
1798	
1799	        if not self.spec_algorithm.is_none():
1800	            # A loose bound that err towards safety
1801	            server_args = get_global_server_args()
1802	            thresh = server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens + (
1803	                (server_args.speculative_eagle_topk or 1)
1804	                * (server_args.speculative_num_steps or 1)
1805	            )
1806	            return sum(
1807	                1 for req in requests if ((req.seqlen + thresh) % page_size) <= thresh
1808	            )
1809	
1810	        # In the decoding phase, the length of a request's KV cache should be
1811	        # the total length of the request minus 1
1812	        return (
1813	            sum(1 for req in requests if req.seqlen % page_size == 0)

1816	        )

1818	    def check_decode_mem(
1819	        self, buf_multiplier=1, selected_indices: Optional[List[int]] = None

1822	            self.new_page_count_next_decode(selected_indices)

1825	        )

1827	        evict_from_tree_cache(self.tree_cache, num_tokens)

1830	    def retract_all(self, server_args: ServerArgs):
1831	        retracted_reqs = self.reqs
1832	        for idx in range(len(self.reqs)):
1833	            self.release_req(idx, len(self.reqs) - idx, server_args)

1837	
1838	    def retract_decode(
1839	        self,
1840	        server_args: ServerArgs,
1841	        buf_multiplier: int = 1,
1842	    ) -> Tuple[List[Req], float, List[Req]]:
1843	        """Retract the decoding requests when there is not enough memory."""
1844	        sorted_indices = list(range(len(self.reqs)))
1845	
1846	        # TODO(lsyin): improve retraction policy for radix cache
1847	        # For spec decoding, filter_batch API can only filter
1848	        # requests from the back, so we can only retract from the back.
1849	        # TODO(sang): Clean up finish path and support better retract
1850	        # policy.
1851	        if not server_args.speculative_algorithm:
1852	            sorted_indices.sort(
1853	                key=lambda i: (
1854	                    len(self.reqs[i].output_ids),
1855	                    -len(self.reqs[i].origin_input_ids),
1856	                ),
1857	                reverse=True,
1858	            )
1859	
1860	        retracted_reqs = []
1861	        first_iter = True
1862	        while first_iter or (
1863	            not self.check_decode_mem(
1864	                selected_indices=sorted_indices, buf_multiplier=buf_multiplier
1865	            )
1866	        ):
1867	            if len(sorted_indices) == 1:
1868	                # Always keep at least one request
1869	                break
1870	
1871	            first_iter = False
1872	            idx = sorted_indices.pop()
1873	            req = self.reqs[idx]
1874	            retracted_reqs.append(req)
1875	            # release memory and don't insert into the tree because we need the space instantly
1876	            self.release_req(idx, len(sorted_indices), server_args)
1877	
1878	        if len(sorted_indices) <= 1 and not self.check_decode_mem(
1879	            selected_indices=sorted_indices, buf_multiplier=buf_multiplier
1880	        ):
1881	            # Retracting loops ends and still not enough memory
1882	            raise ValueError(
1883	                "Out of memory even after retracting all other requests in the decode batch."
1884	            )
1885	
1886	        self.filter_batch(keep_indices=sorted_indices)
1887	
1888	        # Reqs in batch are filtered
1889	        total_decoded_tokens = sum(len(r.output_ids) for r in self.reqs)
1890	        total_max_new_tokens = sum(r.sampling_params.max_new_tokens for r in self.reqs)
1891	
1892	        new_estimate_ratio = (
1893	            total_decoded_tokens
1894	            + envs.SGLANG_RETRACT_DECODE_STEPS.get() * len(self.reqs)
1895	        ) / (
1896	            total_max_new_tokens + 1
1897	        )  # avoid zero division
1898	        new_estimate_ratio = min(1.0, new_estimate_ratio)
1899	
1900	        return retracted_reqs, new_estimate_ratio, []
1901

568	            )

142	    def handle_max_mamba_cache(self: ModelRunner, total_rest_memory):
143	        config = self.mambaish_config
144	        server_args = self.server_args
145	        assert config is not None
146	
147	        # reserve the memory for the intermediate mamba states used for spec dec
148	        if not self.spec_algorithm.is_none():
149	            assert server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens is not None
150	            assert server_args.max_running_requests is not None
151	
152	            max_running_requests = server_args.max_running_requests // (
153	                self.dp_size if server_args.enable_dp_attention else 1
154	            )
155	            mamba_state_intermediate_size = (
156	                config.mamba2_cache_params.mamba_cache_per_req
157	                * max_running_requests
158	                * server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens
159	            )
160	            total_rest_memory = total_rest_memory - (
161	                mamba_state_intermediate_size / (1 << 30)
162	            )
163	
164	        if (
165	            server_args.disable_radix_cache
166	            or server_args.max_mamba_cache_size is not None
167	        ):
168	            # with disable radix cache, sets the max_mamba_cache_size based on the max_running_requests
169	            if server_args.max_mamba_cache_size is None:
170	                if server_args.max_running_requests is not None:
171	                    server_args.max_mamba_cache_size = server_args.max_running_requests
172	                else:
173	                    server_args.max_mamba_cache_size = 512
174	            server_args.max_mamba_cache_size = server_args.max_mamba_cache_size // (
175	                server_args.dp_size if server_args.enable_dp_attention else 1
176	            )
177	        else:
178	            assert config.mamba2_cache_params.mamba_cache_per_req > 0
179	
180	            # allocate the memory based on the ratio between mamba state memory vs. full kv cache memory
181	            # solve the equations:
182	            # 1. mamba_state_memory + full_kv_cache_memory == total_rest_memory
183	            # 2. mamba_state_memory / full_kv_cache_memory == server_args.mamba_full_memory_ratio
184	            mamba_state_memory_raw = (
185	                total_rest_memory
186	                * server_args.mamba_full_memory_ratio
187	                / (1 + server_args.mamba_full_memory_ratio)
188	            )
189	            # calculate the max_mamba_cache_size based on the given total mamba memory
190	            server_args.max_mamba_cache_size = int(
191	                (mamba_state_memory_raw * (1 << 30))
192	                // config.mamba2_cache_params.mamba_cache_per_req
193	            )
194	
195	        mamba_state_memory = (
196	            server_args.max_mamba_cache_size
197	            * config.mamba2_cache_params.mamba_cache_per_req
198	            / (1 << 30)
199	        )
200	        return total_rest_memory - mamba_state_memory
201	
202	    def set_num_tokens_hybrid_swa(self: ModelRunner):
203	        page_size = self.server_args.page_size
204	        if "MiMoV2MTP" in self.model_config.hf_config.architectures:
205	            assert self.is_draft_worker
206	            # MiMoV2MTP uses SWA, so set full KV cache to 0
207	            self.full_max_total_num_tokens = 0
208	            self.swa_max_total_num_tokens = (
209	                self.max_total_num_tokens // page_size * page_size
210	            )
211	            self.max_total_num_tokens = self.swa_max_total_num_tokens
212	        else:
213	            assert self.sliding_window_size is not None and self.sliding_window_size > 0
214	            full_layers_num = len(self.model_config.full_attention_layer_ids)
215	            swa_layers_num = len(self.model_config.swa_attention_layer_ids)
216	
217	            # Algorithm:
218	            # Existing max_total_num_tokens is per layer and assume all layers have the same number of tokens.
219	            # - Find total # of tokens available across layers.
220	            # - Calculate full_max_total_num_tokens and swa_max_total_num_tokens based on the given swa_full_tokens_ratio.
221	            total_tokens = (
222	                self.max_total_num_tokens * self.model_config.num_hidden_layers
223	            )
224	            swa_full_tokens_ratio = self.server_args.swa_full_tokens_ratio
225	
226	            # Solve the equations:
227	            # 1. swa_max_total_num_tokens * swa_layers_num + full_max_total_num_tokens * full_layers_num == total_tokens
228	            # 2. full_max_total_num_tokens * swa_full_tokens_ratio == swa_max_total_num_tokens
229	            denominator = swa_full_tokens_ratio * swa_layers_num + full_layers_num
230	            self.full_max_total_num_tokens = int(total_tokens / denominator)
231	            self.swa_max_total_num_tokens = int(
232	                self.full_max_total_num_tokens * swa_full_tokens_ratio
233	            )
234	
235	            self.full_max_total_num_tokens = (
236	                self.full_max_total_num_tokens // page_size * page_size
237	            )
238	            self.swa_max_total_num_tokens = (
239	                self.swa_max_total_num_tokens // page_size * page_size
240	            )
241	
242	            self.max_total_num_tokens = self.full_max_total_num_tokens
243	
244	        logger.info(
245	            f"Use sliding window memory pool. full_layer_tokens={self.full_max_total_num_tokens}, swa_layer_tokens={self.swa_max_total_num_tokens}"
246	        )
247	
248	    def init_memory_pool(self: ModelRunner, total_gpu_memory: int):
249	        max_num_reqs = self.server_args.max_running_requests
250	        max_total_tokens = self.server_args.max_total_tokens
251	        self.max_total_num_tokens = self.profile_max_num_token(total_gpu_memory)
252	
253	        if max_num_reqs is None:
254	            max_num_reqs = min(
255	                max(
256	                    int(
257	                        self.max_total_num_tokens / self.model_config.context_len * 512
258	                    ),
259	                    2048,
260	                ),
261	                4096,
262	            )
263	
264	        if self.mambaish_config is not None:
265	            additional_ratio = 0
266	            if self.server_args.enable_mamba_extra_buffer():
267	                if not self.spec_algorithm.is_none():
268	                    additional_ratio = MAMBA_CACHE_V2_ADDITIONAL_RATIO_NO_OVERLAP
269	                else:
270	                    additional_ratio = MAMBA_CACHE_V2_ADDITIONAL_RATIO_OVERLAP
271	            if self.server_args.disable_radix_cache:
272	                ratio = 1
273	            else:
274	                ratio = MAMBA_CACHE_SIZE_MAX_RUNNING_REQUESTS_RATIO + additional_ratio
275	            max_num_reqs = min(
276	                max_num_reqs, self.server_args.max_mamba_cache_size // ratio
277	            )
278	            # for dp attention, we need control the max_num_reqs for speculative decoding mamba space
279	            if (
280	                not self.spec_algorithm.is_none()
281	                and self.server_args.enable_dp_attention
282	            ):
283	                max_num_reqs = min(
284	                    max_num_reqs, self.server_args.max_running_requests // self.dp_size
285	                )
286	
287	        if not self.spec_algorithm.is_none():
288	            if self.is_draft_worker:
289	                self.max_total_num_tokens = self.server_args.draft_runner_cache_size
290	                max_num_reqs = self.server_args.max_num_reqs
291	            else:
292	                self.server_args.draft_runner_cache_size = self.max_total_num_tokens
293	                self.server_args.max_num_reqs = max_num_reqs
294	
295	        if max_total_tokens is not None:
296	            if max_total_tokens > self.max_total_num_tokens:
297	                logging.warning(
298	                    f"max_total_tokens={max_total_tokens} is larger than the profiled value "
299	                    f"{self.max_total_num_tokens}. "
300	                    f"Use the profiled value instead."
301	                )
302	            self.max_total_num_tokens = min(self.max_total_num_tokens, max_total_tokens)
303	
304	        self.max_total_num_tokens = (
305	            self.max_total_num_tokens
306	            // self.server_args.page_size
307	            * self.server_args.page_size
308	        )
309	        # different pp rank may have different num of layers, so we need to reduce the max_total_num_tokens
310	        if self.pp_size > 1:
311	            tensor = torch.tensor(self.max_total_num_tokens, dtype=torch.int64)
312	            torch.distributed.all_reduce(
313	                tensor,
314	                op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.MIN,
315	                group=get_world_group().cpu_group,
316	            )
317	            self.max_total_num_tokens = tensor.item()
318	
319	        # create token size for hybrid cache
320	        if self.is_hybrid_swa:
321	            self.set_num_tokens_hybrid_swa()
322	
323	        if self.max_total_num_tokens <= 0:
324	            raise RuntimeError(
325	                f"Not enough memory. Please try to increase --mem-fraction-static. "
326	                f"Current value: {self.server_args.mem_fraction_static=}"
327	            )
328	
329	        # Initialize req_to_token_pool
330	        if self.req_to_token_pool is None:
331	            # FIXME(lsyin): this is the temporary fix for the context length issue when using speculative decoding
332	            extra_max_context_len = 4
333	            if self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens is not None:
334	                extra_max_context_len += self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens
335	
336	            if self.server_args.disaggregation_mode == "decode":
337	                from sglang.srt.disaggregation.decode import (
338	                    DecodeReqToTokenPool,
339	                    HybridMambaDecodeReqToTokenPool,
340	                )
341	
342	                # subscribe memory for pre-allocated requests
343	                # if max_num_reqs <= 32, we pre-allocate 2x requests
344	                pre_alloc_size = max_num_reqs * 2 if max_num_reqs <= 32 else 0
345	                if config := self.mambaish_config:
346	                    self.req_to_token_pool = HybridMambaDecodeReqToTokenPool(
347	                        size=max_num_reqs,
348	                        max_context_len=self.model_config.context_len
349	                        + extra_max_context_len,
350	                        device=self.device,
351	                        enable_memory_saver=self.server_args.enable_memory_saver,
352	                        cache_params=config.mamba2_cache_params,
353	                        speculative_num_draft_tokens=self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens,
354	                        enable_mamba_extra_buffer=self.server_args.enable_mamba_extra_buffer(),
355	                        pre_alloc_size=pre_alloc_size,
356	                    )
357	                else:
358	                    self.req_to_token_pool = DecodeReqToTokenPool(
359	                        size=max_num_reqs,
360	                        max_context_len=self.model_config.context_len
361	                        + extra_max_context_len,
362	                        device=self.device,
363	                        enable_memory_saver=self.server_args.enable_memory_saver,
364	                        pre_alloc_size=pre_alloc_size,
365	                    )

283	    def mamba2_layer_cache(self, layer_id: int):
284	        return self.mamba_cache.at_layer_idx(layer_id)
285	
286	    def available_size(self):
287	        return len(self.free_slots)
288	
289	    def alloc(self, need_size: int) -> Optional[torch.Tensor]:
290	        if need_size > len(self.free_slots):
291	            return None
292	
293	        select_index = self.free_slots[:need_size]
294	        self.free_slots = self.free_slots[need_size:]
295	        # clear at alloc time, fill allocated slots with zeros
296	        for i in range(len(self.mamba_cache.conv)):
297	            self.mamba_cache.conv[i][:, select_index] = 0
298	        if self.mamba_cache.temporal is not None:
299	            self.mamba_cache.temporal[:, select_index] = 0
300	
301	        return select_index
302	
303	    def free(self, free_index: torch.Tensor):
304	        if free_index.numel() == 0:
305	            return
306	        self.free_slots = torch.cat((self.free_slots, free_index))
307	
308	    def clear(self):
309	        self.free_slots = torch.arange(self.size, dtype=torch.int64, device=self.device)
310	
311	    def copy_from(self, src_index: torch.Tensor, dst_index: torch.Tensor):
312	        for i in range(len(self.mamba_cache.conv)):
313	            self.mamba_cache.conv[i][:, dst_index] = self.mamba_cache.conv[i][
314	                :, src_index
315	            ]
316	        if self.mamba_cache.temporal is not None:
317	            self.mamba_cache.temporal[:, dst_index] = self.mamba_cache.temporal[
318	                :, src_index
319	            ]
320	        return
321	
322	    def fork_from(self, src_index: torch.Tensor) -> Optional[torch.Tensor]:
323	        dst_index = self.alloc(1)
324	        if dst_index == None:
325	            return None
326	        self.copy_from(src_index, dst_index)
327	        return dst_index
328	
329	    def get_contiguous_buf_infos(self):
330	        state_tensors = []
331	        for field in vars(self.mamba_cache):
332	            value = getattr(self.mamba_cache, field)
333	            if isinstance(value, list):
334	                state_tensors.extend(value)
335	            else:
336	                state_tensors.append(value)
337	        data_ptrs, data_lens, item_lens = [], [], []
338	
339	        for _, state_tensor in enumerate(state_tensors):
340	            data_ptrs += [
341	                state_tensor[i].data_ptr() for i in range(self.num_mamba_layers)
342	            ]
343	            data_lens += [state_tensor[i].nbytes for i in range(self.num_mamba_layers)]
344	            item_lens += [
345	                state_tensor[i][0].nbytes for i in range(self.num_mamba_layers)
346	            ]
347	        return data_ptrs, data_lens, item_lens

600	                f"Capture draft extend cuda graph end. Time elapsed: {time.perf_counter() - tic:.2f} s. mem usage={(before_mem - after_mem):.2f} GB. avail mem={after_mem:.2f} GB."
601	            )

603	    @property
604	    def draft_model_runner(self):
605	        return self.model_runner
606	
607	    def forward_batch_generation(self, batch: ScheduleBatch) -> GenerationBatchResult:
608	        """Run speculative decoding forward.
609	
610	        NOTE: Many states of batch is modified as you go through. It is not guaranteed that
611	        the final output batch have the same state as the input.
612	
613	        Args:
614	            batch: The batch to run forward. The state of the batch is modified as it runs.
615	        Returns:
616	            A tuple of the final logit output of the target model, next tokens accepted,
617	            the batch id (used for overlap schedule), and number of accepted tokens.
618	        """
619	        if batch.forward_mode.is_extend() or batch.is_extend_in_batch:
620	            logits_output, next_token_ids, seq_lens_cpu = self.forward_target_extend(
621	                batch
622	            )
623	            with self.draft_tp_context(
624	                self.draft_model_runner.tp_group
625	            ), speculative_moe_backend_context(), speculative_moe_a2a_backend_context():
626	                self.forward_draft_extend(
627	                    batch, logits_output.hidden_states, next_token_ids, seq_lens_cpu
628	                )
629	            self._prev_spec_mode = None
630	            return GenerationBatchResult(
631	                logits_output=logits_output,
632	                next_token_ids=next_token_ids,
633	                num_accepted_tokens=0,
634	                can_run_cuda_graph=False,
635	            )
636	        else:
637	            mode = getattr(batch, "_spec_mode", None)
638	
639	            if mode == SpecMode.NO_SPEC:
640	                self._prev_spec_mode = mode
641	                return self._forward_no_spec(batch)
642	
643	            # Spec mode (D5/D7 or legacy fixed config). Apply per-mode params
644	            # before the draft/verify path consumes self.* / server_args.*.
645	            if mode is not None:
646	                self._apply_spec_config(mode)
647	            self._prev_spec_mode = mode
648	
649	            with self.draft_tp_context(
650	                self.draft_model_runner.tp_group
651	            ), speculative_moe_backend_context(), speculative_moe_a2a_backend_context():
652	                with _profile_region("EW_draft"):
653	                    spec_info = self.draft(batch)
654	            with _profile_region("EW_verify_total"):
655	                logits_output, verify_output, model_worker_batch, can_run_cuda_graph = (
656	                    self.verify(batch, spec_info)
657	                )
658	
659	            with self.draft_tp_context(
660	                self.draft_model_runner.tp_group
661	            ), speculative_moe_backend_context(), speculative_moe_a2a_backend_context():
662	                # NOTE: We should use `check_forward_draft_extend_after_decode`
663	                # when DP attention is enabled, but it is slow. Skip it for now.
664	                if (
665	                    self.server_args.enable_dp_attention
666	                    or batch.spec_info.verified_id.shape[0] > 0
667	                ):
668	                    # decode is not finished
669	                    with _profile_region("EW_draft_extend_after_decode"):
670	                        self.forward_draft_extend_after_decode(batch)
671	
672	            # Tell metrics layer the actual per-req draft capacity for this
673	            # step. self.speculative_num_draft_tokens has already been swapped
674	            # by _apply_spec_config to D5/D7's dtn (or unchanged for static
675	            # spec). Subtracting 1 strips the user_4813494d token to get pure draft.
676	            return GenerationBatchResult(
677	                logits_output=logits_output,
678	                next_token_ids=verify_output.verified_id,
679	                num_accepted_tokens=sum(verify_output.accept_length_per_req_cpu),
680	                accept_length_per_req_cpu=verify_output.accept_length_per_req_cpu,
681	                can_run_cuda_graph=can_run_cuda_graph,
682	                max_draft_per_req=max(self.speculative_num_draft_tokens - 1, 0),
683	            )
684	
685	    def _apply_spec_config(self, mode: SpecMode) -> None:
686	        """Switch worker spec config (topk / steps / dtn) and active draft graph.
687	
688	        No-op if dynamic mode disabled or mode is the same as last applied. The
689	        draft attn_backend is shared across modes (sized to ``_max_spec_steps``
690	        at init); only the cuda-graph runner buffers differ.
691	        """
692	        cfg = self.spec_mode_config
693	        if not cfg.enabled:
694	            return
695	        if mode == SpecMode.MARS_D5:
696	            new_topk, new_steps, new_dtn = cfg.d5_topk, cfg.d5_steps, cfg.d5_dtn
697	            new_runner = self.cuda_graph_runner_d5
698	            new_theta = cfg.effective_d5_theta()
699	        elif mode == SpecMode.MARS_D7:
700	            new_topk, new_steps, new_dtn = cfg.d7_topk, cfg.d7_steps, cfg.d7_dtn
701	            new_runner = self.cuda_graph_runner_d7
702	            new_theta = cfg.effective_d7_theta()
703	        else:
704	            return  # NO_SPEC handled separately
705	
706	        # Steady-state fast path: D5 -> D5 / D7 -> D7 are the vast majority of
707	        # decode steps once the bs settles. _applied_spec_mode is None on the
708	        # very first call (forces full re-bind so cuda_graph_runner is correct
709	        # even if self.* defaults already match the requested mode).
710	        if self._applied_spec_mode == mode:
711	            return
712	
713	        self.topk = new_topk
714	        self.speculative_num_steps = new_steps
715	        self.speculative_num_draft_tokens = new_dtn
716	        self.server_args.speculative_eagle_topk = new_topk
717	        self.server_args.speculative_num_steps = new_steps
718	        self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens = new_dtn
719	        self.cuda_graph_runner = new_runner

572	        self, req: Req, has_chunked_req: bool, truncation_align_size: Optional[int]

676	    def preempt_to_schedule(self, req: Req, server_args: ServerArgs) -> bool:
677	        """
678	        Preempt running requests to serve the new request if the priority threshold is met and token count sum is verified.
679	        Returns True if preemption was committed, and the new request can be scheduled.
680	        """
681	        # Iterate running requests to find preemptible requests
682	        priority_sign = 1 if server_args.schedule_low_priority_values_first else -1
683	
684	        valid_running_reqs = (
685	            r for r in self.running_batch.reqs if r not in self.preempt_list
686	        )
687	
688	        sorted_valid_running_reqs = sorted(
689	            valid_running_reqs,
690	            key=lambda x: (
691	                x.priority * (-priority_sign),
692	                -x.time_stats.wait_queue_entry_time,
693	            ),
694	        )
695	
696	        preemptible_reqs = []
697	        min_tokens_to_remove = (
698	            req.extend_input_len
699	            + min(req.sampling_params.max_new_tokens, CLIP_MAX_NEW_TOKENS)
700	            - self.rem_total_tokens
701	        )
702	        for running_req in sorted_valid_running_reqs:
703	            # Priority difference needs to meet the threshold to be preemptible.
704	            priority_diff = (req.priority - running_req.priority) * (-priority_sign)
705	
706	            if priority_diff > self.priority_scheduling_preemption_threshold:
707	                preemptible_reqs.append(running_req)
708	                min_tokens_to_remove -= self._get_running_request_total_token_offset(
709	                    running_req
710	                )
711	                if min_tokens_to_remove <= 0:
712	                    break
713	            else:
714	                break
715	
716	        # Check max token count limit can be met
717	        if len(preemptible_reqs) == 0 or min_tokens_to_remove > 0:
718	            return False
719	
720	        # Preempt running requests. Release allocated resources for immediate usage.
721	        preemptible_reqs = set(preemptible_reqs)
722	        keep_indices = []
723	        release_counter = 0
724	        for i, running_req in enumerate(self.running_batch.reqs):
725	            if running_req in preemptible_reqs:
726	                self.rem_total_token_offset -= (
727	                    self._get_running_request_total_token_offset(running_req)
728	                )
729	                release_counter += 1
730	                self.running_batch.release_req(
731	                    i, len(self.running_batch.reqs) - release_counter, server_args
732	                )
733	            else:
734	                keep_indices.append(i)
735	        self.running_batch.filter_batch(keep_indices=keep_indices)
736	        self.preempt_list.extend(preemptible_reqs)
737	        return True

1495	        config = self.model_config.hf_config

1497	            return config

1500	    @property

1502	        return self.mamba2_config or self.hybrid_gdn_config or self.kimi_linear_config or self.minicpm_hybrid_config

1505	        if self.server_args.enable_torch_compile:

47	def mamba2_state_dtype() -> Mamba2StateDType:
48	    dtype_map = {

50	        "bfloat16": torch.bfloat16,
51	    }

55	
56	@dataclass(kw_only=True, frozen=True)
57	class BaseLinearStateParams(ABC):
58	    dtype: Mamba2StateDType = field(default_factory=mamba2_state_dtype)
59	    layers: list[int]
60	
61	    @property
62	    def mamba_cache_per_req(self) -> int:
63	        conv_numel = int(
64	            np.sum([np.prod(conv_shape) for conv_shape in self.shape.conv])
65	        )
66	
67	        ssm_numel = int(np.prod(self.shape.temporal))
68	        return (
69	            conv_numel * self.dtype.conv.itemsize
70	            + ssm_numel * self.dtype.temporal.itemsize
71	        ) * len(self.layers)
72	
73	
74	@dataclass(kw_only=True, frozen=True)
75	class Mamba2StateShape:
76	    conv: list[tuple[int, int]]
77	    temporal: tuple[int, int, int]
78	
79	    intermediate_size: int
80	    conv_dim: int
81	    ssm_state_size: int
82	    num_heads: int
83	    head_dim: int
84	    state_size: int
85	    conv_kernel: int
86	
87	    @staticmethod
88	    def create(
89	        *,
90	        tp_world_size: int,
91	        intermediate_size: int,
92	        n_groups: int,
93	        num_heads: int,
94	        head_dim: int,
95	        state_size: int,
96	        conv_kernel: int,
97	    ) -> "Mamba2StateShape":
98	        # if n_groups is not divisible by world_size, need to extend the shards
99	        # to ensure all groups needed by a head is sharded along with it
100	        if n_groups % tp_world_size != 0:
101	            extra_groups = extra_groups_for_head_shards(n_groups, tp_world_size)
102	            n_groups += extra_groups
103	        # heads and n_groups are TP-ed
104	        conv_dim = intermediate_size + 2 * n_groups * state_size
105	
106	        # contiguous along 'dim' axis
107	        conv_state_shape = divide(conv_dim, tp_world_size), conv_kernel - 1
108	
109	        # These are not TP-ed as they depend on A, dt_bias, D
110	        # - they are typically small
111	        #   e.g., QWen3-Next: (32, 128, 128)
112	        temporal_state_shape = (divide(num_heads, tp_world_size), head_dim, state_size)
113	        return Mamba2StateShape(
114	            conv=[conv_state_shape],
115	            temporal=temporal_state_shape,
116	            intermediate_size=intermediate_size,
117	            conv_dim=conv_dim,
118	            ssm_state_size=state_size,
119	            num_heads=num_heads,
120	            head_dim=head_dim,
121	            state_size=state_size,
122	            conv_kernel=conv_kernel,
123	        )
124	
125	
126	@dataclass(kw_only=True, frozen=True)
127	class Mamba2CacheParams(BaseLinearStateParams):
128	    shape: Mamba2StateShape
129

134	    
135	    num_heads: int
136	    head_dim: int
137	    state_size: int
138	    
139	    @staticmethod
140	    def create(
141	        *,
142	        tp_world_size: int,
143	        num_heads: int,
144	        head_dim: int,
145	        state_size: int,
146	    ) -> "SimpleGLAStateShape":
147	        temporal_state_shape = (divide(num_heads, tp_world_size), head_dim, state_size)
148	        return SimpleGLAStateShape(

150	            num_heads=num_heads,
151	            head_dim=head_dim,

153	        )

142	        return self.has_sparse_config and (self.mixer_types is None or any(mt == "minicpm4" for mt in self.mixer_types))
143	
144	    @property

112	    def profile_max_num_token(self: ModelRunner, total_gpu_memory: int):
113	        available_gpu_memory = get_available_gpu_memory(
114	            self.device,
115	            self.gpu_id,
116	            distributed=get_world_group().world_size > 1,
117	            cpu_group=get_world_group().cpu_group,
118	        )
119	
120	        # Get the number of layers used for KV cache calculation
121	        if self.is_draft_worker:
122	            num_layers = getattr(
123	                self.model_config.hf_config,
124	                "num_nextn_predict_layers",
125	                self.num_effective_layers,
126	            )
127	        elif mambaish := self.mambaish_config:
128	            num_layers = len(mambaish.full_attention_layer_ids)
129	        else:
130	            num_layers = self.num_effective_layers
131	
132	        cell_size = self.get_cell_size_per_token(num_layers)
133	
134	        rest_memory = available_gpu_memory - total_gpu_memory * (
135	            1 - self.mem_fraction_static
136	        )
137	        if self.mambaish_config is not None:
138	            rest_memory = self.handle_max_mamba_cache(rest_memory)
139	
140	        return int(rest_memory * (1 << 30)) // cell_size
141	
142	    def handle_max_mamba_cache(self: ModelRunner, total_rest_memory):
143	        config = self.mambaish_config
144	        server_args = self.server_args
145	        assert config is not None
146	
147	        # reserve the memory for the intermediate mamba states used for spec dec
148	        if not self.spec_algorithm.is_none():
149	            assert server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens is not None

> AGENT

调查完成。下面是结论与依据。

---

## 一句话结论

**`intermediate_ssm_state_cache` 这块按 `mamba_cache_per_req × max_running_requests × max_dtn` 一次性预分配的 mamba 中间状态 buffer，在 dynamic spec 模式下被按 D7 的 max_dtn 而不是 D5 的 dtn 分配，从 `total_rest_memory` 直接扣，把 KV pool 砍到不到一半，所以 prefill 还没堆到目标就 OOM。** 这跟 `--max-running-requests` 的设置不是同一个轴 —— 即使把 max_running_requests 抬到 64，这块预留也会按比例放大，KV pool 反而更吃紧。

---

## 关键证据（同条件不同 spec 配置的启动日志对比）

| 配置 | `max_running_requests` | `ssm_state` | `intermediate_ssm` | `max_total_num_tokens` | `available_gpu_mem` |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| no-spec（`/tmp/sglang_server.log`） | 64 | 3.05 GB | — | **6,769,068** | 13.09 GB |
| dynamic spec D5/D7 dtn=5/7（`/tmp/sglang_dynamic_smoke.log`） | 64 | 3.05 GB | **21.33 GB** | **3,480,162** | 7.88 GB |
| dynamic spec D5/D7 dtn=7/9（`/tmp/sglang_p0off2.log` 等） | 36 | 1.73 GB | **15.61 GB** | 4,414,050 | 8.65 GB |

KV pool 缩了 49%，可用 GPU 也少了 5+ GB，**全部被 `intermediate_ssm` 吃掉**。

mamba 槽位独立、固定 = `max_running_requests`（`disable_radix_cache=True` 路径强制，见 `model_runner_kv_cache_mixin.py:169-176`），所以**调高 `max_running_requests` 同时会让 `intermediate_ssm` 按比例放大**。当前 `start_eagle.sh` 是 `D7_DTN=11`，要跑 64 running 时，按上面 ssm_state≈3.05 GB 的换算，`intermediate_ssm ≈ 3.05×11 ≈ 33.5 GB`，加 NVFP4 权重和 cuda graph 直接撑爆。

## 代码路径

1. **bump → init_memory_pool 同时机** (`scheduler.py:560-589`)：dynamic spec 模式下 `init_tp_model_worker()` 调用前会把 `server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens` 升到 `max(d5_dtn, d7_dtn)`。这次 bump 是**必要**的，draft cuda graph buffer 需要这个大小。

2. **`handle_max_mamba_cache` 在 bump 期间运行** (`model_runner_kv_cache_mixin.py:147-162`)：
   ```python
   mamba_state_intermediate_size = (
       mamba_cache_per_req * max_running_requests
       * server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens   # ← bump 后的 max_dtn=11
   )
   total_rest_memory -= mamba_state_intermediate_size / (1<<30)
   ```
   这块预留**直接从 `rest_memory` 里扣**，剩下的才用来算 `max_total_num_tokens`。

3. **MambaPool 实分配** (`memory_pool.py:215-251`)：`intermediate_ssm_state_cache` 形状 = `(num_layers, max_running_requests+1, dtn, ...)`、`fp32`。MiniCPM SimpleGLA 24 层、`mamba_ssm_dtype='float32'`，每 req 47.7 MB 全量保留。

4. **消费侧** (`hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py:2319-2331`)：target verify 路径里 `ht_buf = intermediate_ssm[cache_idx, :batch_size, :draft_token_num]`。这块是 GLA fused recurrent kernel 写 ht 的目的地，确实要预留 —— 但**当前是按 max_dtn × max_running_requests 一次性分配，不是按 active mode 缩**。

5. **bump 之后 restore** (`scheduler.py:596-608`)：`server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens` 在 cuda graph capture 后被 restore 到 CLI 值（=7），所以后面 `decode_mem_cache_buf_multiplier` 是按 D5 算的（13），而 D7 active 时实际每步要 21 个 slot —— 这是**第二个不一致**（在 decode 阶段触发偏晚的 retract），但不是用户报的"prefill 中间满"现象的直接原因。

## 触发现场

- **mamba 槽满**：`memory_pool.py:434-436` `assert mid is not None, f"Not enough space for mamba cache..."` —— 当 `running_bs + 新 prefill > mamba_pool.size = max_running_requests`。
- **KV 槽不够**：`schedule_policy.py:596-597` 在 `PrefillAdder.add_one_req` 里 `total_tokens >= rem_total_tokens` → 返回 `AddReqResult.NO_TOKEN`，prefill admission 提前停。这块 `rem_total_tokens` 来自缩水后的 `token_to_kv_pool_allocator.available_size()`。

## 可行修复方向（按性价比）

1. **降 `EAGLE_D7_DTN`**（最简单）：从 11 调到 9 或 7。intermediate 立即减 18-36%。代价是 D7 加速比下降，要 trade-off bench。
2. **拆 D5 / D7 的 intermediate buffer**：D7 设计上只在 `bs≤1` 启用（已有 `cap_max_bs=1` 标记，`eagle_worker.py:559`），但 `MambaPool` 的 intermediate 没分两份，第二维仍按 `max_running_requests+1` 分配。如果把 D7 路径独立成 `(num_layers, 1+1, d7_dtn, ...)`、D5 路径维持现样按 `d5_dtn` 分配，可以把 intermediate 从 `(max_running_requests+1)*max_dtn` 砍到 `(max_running_requests+1)*d5_dtn + 2*d7_dtn`，节约接近 `(max_running_requests-1)*(d7_dtn-d5_dtn)*mamba_cache_per_req`，对 64 reqs/D7=11 这一档约 9 GB。改动点在 `MambaPool.__init__` 和 `hybrid_linear_attn_backend.py:2325` 的 `get_speculative_mamba2_params_all_layers()` 两处。
3. **保持 max_running_requests=36**：当前 start_eagle.sh 的事实选择，放弃 64-running 目标。只要 dynamic spec 还在，提高 max_running_requests 就会带来 ~`max_running_requests × max_dtn × mamba_cache_per_req` 的额外内存负担。

要我先做方向 1 的扫描（看不同 D7_dtn 下的 accept-length / 端到端速度）还是直接改方向 2？

> AGENT

实测数据铁实，证实结论。**当前 max_running=36 配置下 `intermediate_ssm = 19.08 GB`，按 D7 `max_dtn=11` 一次性分配。** 如果改 max_running=64，会膨胀到 ~33.5 GB，直接撑爆。

把 D7 单独按 `bs=1` 分配的方案 2 节约（max_running=36 → 节约 ~6 GB；max_running=64 → 节约 ~11 GB）。

1518	            intermediate_state_cache = mamba_cache_params.intermediate_ssm
1519	            intermediate_conv_window_cache = (
1520	                mamba_cache_params.intermediate_conv_window[0]
1521	            )
1522	            has_initial_states = torch.ones(
1523	                seq_len // forward_batch.spec_info.draft_token_num,
1524	                dtype=torch.bool,
1525	                device=forward_batch.input_ids.device,
1526	            )
1527	            intermediate_state_indices = torch.arange(
1528	                cache_indices.shape[0], dtype=torch.int32, device=cache_indices.device
1529	            )
1530	        else:
1531	            has_initial_states = forward_batch.extend_prefix_lens > 0
1532	
1533	        if is_target_verify:
1534	            batch_size = seq_len // forward_batch.spec_info.draft_token_num
1535	            draft_token_num = forward_batch.spec_info.draft_token_num
1536	            mixed_qkv_reshaped = mixed_qkv.view(
1537	                batch_size, draft_token_num, -1
1538	            ).transpose(1, 2)
1539	            mixed_qkv_processed = causal_conv1d_update(
1540	                mixed_qkv_reshaped,
1541	                conv_states,
1542	                conv_weights,
1543	                bias,
1544	                activation,
1545	                conv_state_indices=cache_indices[:batch_size],
1546	                intermediate_conv_window=intermediate_conv_window_cache,
1547	                intermediate_state_indices=intermediate_state_indices[:batch_size],
1548	                [REDACTED],
1549	                retrieve_next_sibling=retrieve_next_sibling,
1550	                [REDACTED],
1551	            )
1552	            mixed_qkv = mixed_qkv_processed.transpose(1, 2).view(seq_len, -1)
1553	        else:
1554	            mixed_qkv = mixed_qkv.transpose(0, 1)
1555	            if (
1556	                forward_batch.mamba_track_mask is not None
1557	                and forward_batch.mamba_track_mask.any()
1558	            ):
1559	                conv_dst = forward_batch.mamba_track_indices
1560	                # Gather all slices at once: [:, track_conv_indices] -> [d, num_masked, slice_len]
1561	                # track_conv_indices is already filtered and clamped in _init_track_conv_indices
1562	                mixed_qkv_to_track = mixed_qkv[
1563	                    :, forward_metadata.track_conv_indices
1564	                ].transpose(0, 1)
1565	                # Apply mask and assign to destinations
1566	                mask_indices = forward_batch.mamba_track_mask.nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
1567	                conv_states[conv_dst[mask_indices]] = mixed_qkv_to_track
1568	
1569	            mixed_qkv = causal_conv1d_fn(
1570	                mixed_qkv,
1571	                conv_weights,
1572	                bias,
1573	                activation=activation,
1574	                conv_states=conv_states,
1575	                has_initial_state=has_initial_states,
1576	                cache_indices=cache_indices,
1577	                query_start_loc=query_start_loc,
1578	                seq_lens_cpu=forward_batch.extend_seq_lens_cpu,
1579	            ).transpose(0, 1)[:seq_len]
1580	
1581	        key_split_dim = key_dim // attn_tp_size
1582	        value_split_dim = value_dim // attn_tp_size
1583	
1584	        query, key, value = torch.split(
1585	            mixed_qkv,
1586	            [key_split_dim, key_split_dim, value_split_dim],
1587	            dim=-1,
1588	        )
1589	
1590	        actual_seq_len = query.shape[0]
1591	        num_heads = query.shape[1] // head_k_dim
1592	        num_value_heads = value.shape[1] // head_v_dim
1593	
1594	        query = query.view(1, actual_seq_len, num_heads, head_k_dim)
1595	        key = key.view(1, actual_seq_len, num_heads, head_k_dim)
1596	        value = value.view(1, actual_seq_len, num_value_heads, head_v_dim)
1597	
1598	        g, beta = fused_gdn_gating(A_log, a, b, dt_bias)
1599

**`MambaPool` 内部持有两块 SpeculativeState（D5 / D7）**，`mamba_cache` 改成 property 按 `_active` 返回；提供 `set_active_spec_buffer("d5"|"d7")` 切换。消费侧（`hybrid_linear_attn_backend` / `mamba.py`）零改动。**EAGLE worker 在 `_apply_spec_config` 切换。** Dynamic spec 关闭时走原单 buffer 路径。

D7 buffer 行数取 `leave_d7_bs + 1`（D7 active 时 bs ∈ [0, leave_d7_bs-1]）。

289	    def alloc(self, need_size: int) -> Optional[torch.Tensor]:
290	        if need_size > len(self.free_slots):
291	            return None
292	
293	        select_index = self.free_slots[:need_size]
294	        self.free_slots = self.free_slots[need_size:]
295	        # clear at alloc time, fill allocated slots with zeros
296	        for i in range(len(self.mamba_cache.conv)):
297	            self.mamba_cache.conv[i][:, select_index] = 0
298	        if self.mamba_cache.temporal is not None:
299	            self.mamba_cache.temporal[:, select_index] = 0
300	
301	        return select_index
302	
303	    def free(self, free_index: torch.Tensor):
304	        if free_index.numel() == 0:
305	            return
306	        self.free_slots = torch.cat((self.free_slots, free_index))
307	
308	    def clear(self):
309	        self.free_slots = torch.arange(self.size, dtype=torch.int64, device=self.device)

312	        for i in range(len(self.mamba_cache.conv)):
313	            self.mamba_cache.conv[i][:, dst_index] = self.mamba_cache.conv[i][
314	                :, src_index
315	            ]

317	            self.mamba_cache.temporal[:, dst_index] = self.mamba_cache.temporal[
318	                :, src_index

322	    def fork_from(self, src_index: torch.Tensor) -> Optional[torch.Tensor]:
323	        dst_index = self.alloc(1)
324	        if dst_index == None:
325	            return None
326	        self.copy_from(src_index, dst_index)
327	        return dst_index

332	            value = getattr(self.mamba_cache, field)
333	            if isinstance(value, list):
334	                state_tensors.extend(value)

336	                state_tensors.append(value)
337	        data_ptrs, data_lens, item_lens = [], [], []
338

342	            ]

344	            item_lens += [
345	                state_tensor[i][0].nbytes for i in range(self.num_mamba_layers)
346	            ]
347	        return data_ptrs, data_lens, item_lens
