```json
  "new_string": "    def __init__(\n        self,\n        *,\n        size: int,\n        spec_state_size: int,\n        cache_params: BaseLinearStateParams,\n        device: str,\n        enable_memory_saver: bool = False,\n        speculative_num_draft_tokens: Optional[int] = None,\n        # Dynamic-spec split-buffer mode (mutually exclusive with the legacy\n        # single-buffer path above). When all three are given, the pool keeps\n        # two SpeculativeState buffers and switches by `set_active_spec_buffer`:\n        #   - \"d5\": (num_layers, spec_state_size+1, spec_d5_dtn, ...)\n        #   - \"d7\": (num_layers, spec_d7_max_bs+1, spec_d7_dtn, ...)\n        # D7 buffer is sized at the per-mode max running batch (D7 only runs\n        # at small bs by design), so the heavy intermediate cache is sized at\n        # D5's dtn instead of max(d5,d7), freeing room in the KV pool.\n        spec_d5_dtn: Optional[int] = None,\n        spec_d7_dtn: Optional[int] = None,\n        spec_d7_max_bs: Optional[int] = None,\n    ):\n        if cache_params is not None and hasattr(cache_params.shape, 'conv') and cache_params.shape.conv:\n            conv_state_shape = cache_params.shape.conv\n            conv_dtype = cache_params.dtype.conv\n        else:\n            conv_state_shape = []\n            conv_dtype = None\n\n        if cache_params is not None:\n            temporal_state_shape = cache_params.shape.temporal\n            ssm_dtype = cache_params.dtype.temporal\n            num_mamba_layers = len(cache_params.layers)\n        else:\n            temporal_state_shape = []\n            ssm_dtype = None\n            num_mamba_layers = 0\n\n        self.memory_saver_adapter = TorchMemorySaverAdapter.create(\n            enable=enable_memory_saver\n        )\n\n        self.size = size\n        self.device = device\n        self._dynamic_spec_split = (\n            spec_d5_dtn is not None\n            and spec_d7_dtn is not None\n            and spec_d7_max_bs is not None\n        )\n        self._spec_buffers: dict = {}\n        self._active_spec_buffer: str = \"default\"\n\n        # for disagg with nvlink\n        self.enable_custom_mem_pool, self.custom_mem_pool, _ = (\n            maybe_init_custom_mem_pool(device=self.device)\n        )\n\n        def _alloc_intermediate(rows: int, dtn: int):\n            ssm = torch.zeros(\n                size=(\n                    num_mamba_layers,\n                    rows,\n                    dtn,\n                    temporal_state_shape[0],\n                    temporal_state_shape[1],\n                    temporal_state_shape[2],\n                ),\n                dtype=ssm_dtype,\n                device=\"cuda\",\n            )\n            conv_win = [\n                torch.zeros(\n                    size=(num_mamba_layers, rows, dtn, conv_shape[0], conv_shape[1]),\n                    dtype=conv_dtype,\n                    device=\"cuda\",\n                )\n                for conv_shape in conv_state_shape\n            ]\n            return ssm, conv_win\n\n        with self.memory_saver_adapter.region(GPU_MEMORY_TYPE_KV_CACHE), (\n            torch.cuda.use_mem_pool(self.custom_mem_pool)\n            if self.enable_custom_mem_pool\n            else nullcontext()\n        ):\n            if conv_state_shape:\n                conv_state = [\n                    torch.zeros(\n                        size=(num_mamba_layers, size + 1) + conv_shape,\n                        dtype=conv_dtype,\n                        device=device,\n                    )\n                    for conv_shape in conv_state_shape\n                ]\n            else:\n                conv_state = []\n\n            if num_mamba_layers > 0:\n                temporal_state = torch.zeros(\n                    size=(num_mamba_layers, size + 1) + temporal_state_shape,\n                    dtype=ssm_dtype,\n                    device=device,\n                )\n                if self._dynamic_spec_split:\n                    # Two intermediate buffers, one per active spec mode. D5\n                    # holds the full (max_running_requests+1) rows; D7 only\n                    # needs `leave_d7_bs+1` rows since D7 mode's running bs\n                    # is bounded by the hysteresis exit threshold.\n                    d5_ssm, d5_conv_win = _alloc_intermediate(\n                        rows=spec_state_size + 1, dtn=spec_d5_dtn\n                    )\n                    d7_ssm, d7_conv_win = _alloc_intermediate(\n                        rows=spec_d7_max_bs + 1, dtn=spec_d7_dtn\n                    )\n                    self._spec_buffers = {\n                        \"d5\": {\"intermediate_ssm\": d5_ssm, \"intermediate_conv_window\": d5_conv_win},\n                        \"d7\": {\"intermediate_ssm\": d7_ssm, \"intermediate_conv_window\": d7_conv_win},\n                    }\n                    self._spec_state_base = {\n                        \"conv\": conv_state,\n                        \"temporal\": temporal_state,\n                    }\n                    self._active_spec_buffer = \"d5\"\n                    self.mamba_cache = self._build_speculative_state(\"d5\")\n                    total_intermediate_ssm = (\n                        get_tensor_size_bytes(d5_ssm)\n                        + get_tensor_size_bytes(d7_ssm)\n                    )\n                    total_intermediate_conv = (\n                        get_tensor_size_bytes(d5_conv_win)\n                        + get_tensor_size_bytes(d7_conv_win)\n                    )\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"Mamba Cache is allocated (dynamic-spec split). \"\n                        f\"max_mamba_cache_size: {size}, \"\n                        f\"conv_state size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(conv_state) / GB:.2f}GB, \"\n                        f\"ssm_state size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(temporal_state) / GB:.2f}GB \"\n                        f\"intermediate_ssm_state_cache size: {total_intermediate_ssm / GB:.2f}GB \"\n                        f\"(D5={get_tensor_size_bytes(d5_ssm)/GB:.2f}GB rows={spec_state_size + 1}*dtn={spec_d5_dtn}, \"\n                        f\"D7={get_tensor_size_bytes(d7_ssm)/GB:.2f}GB rows={spec_d7_max_bs + 1}*dtn={spec_d7_dtn}) \"\n                        f\"intermediate_conv_window_cache size: {total_intermediate_conv / GB:.2f}GB \"\n                    )\n                elif speculative_num_draft_tokens is not None:\n                    # Legacy single-buffer path. Cache intermediate SSM states\n                    # per draft token during target verify.\n                    # Shape: [num_layers, size + 1, speculative_num_draft_tokens, HV, K, V]\n                    intermediate_ssm_state_cache, intermediate_conv_window_cache = (\n                        _alloc_intermediate(\n                            rows=spec_state_size + 1,\n                            dtn=speculative_num_draft_tokens,\n                        )\n                    )\n                    self.mamba_cache = self.SpeculativeState(\n                        conv=conv_state,\n                        temporal=temporal_state,\n                        intermediate_ssm=intermediate_ssm_state_cache,\n                        intermediate_conv_window=intermediate_conv_window_cache,\n                    )\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"Mamba Cache is allocated. \"\n                        f\"max_mamba_cache_size: {size}, \"\n                        f\"conv_state size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(conv_state) / GB:.2f}GB, \"\n                        f\"ssm_state size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(temporal_state) / GB:.2f}GB \"\n                        f\"intermediate_ssm_state_cache size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(intermediate_ssm_state_cache) / GB:.2f}GB \"\n                        f\"intermediate_conv_window_cache size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(intermediate_conv_window_cache) / GB:.2f}GB \"\n                    )\n                else:\n                    self.mamba_cache = self.State(conv=conv_state, temporal=temporal_state)\n                    logger.info(\n                        f\"Mamba Cache is allocated. \"\n                        f\"max_mamba_cache_size: {size}, \"\n                        f\"conv_state size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(conv_state) / GB:.2f}GB, \"\n                        f\"ssm_state size: {get_tensor_size_bytes(temporal_state) / GB:.2f}GB \"\n                    )\n            else:\n                self.mamba_cache = self.State(conv=[], temporal=None)\n                logger.info(\n                    f\"Mamba Cache is not allocated (num_mamba_layers={num_mamba_layers})\"\n                )\n            self.free_slots = torch.arange(\n                self.size, dtype=torch.int64, device=self.device\n            )\n            self.mem_usage = self.mamba_cache.mem_usage_bytes() / GB\n            if self._dynamic_spec_split:\n                # mem_usage_bytes only counts the active SpeculativeState; add\n                # the inactive D7 buffer for an accurate footprint report.\n                inactive_ssm = self._spec_buffers[\"d7\"][\"intermediate_ssm\"]\n                inactive_conv = self._spec_buffers[\"d7\"][\"intermediate_conv_window\"]\n                self.mem_usage += (\n                    get_tensor_size_bytes(inactive_ssm)\n                    + get_tensor_size_bytes(inactive_conv)\n                ) / GB\n            self.num_mamba_layers = num_mamba_layers\n\n    def _build_speculative_state(self, mode: str) -> \"MambaPool.SpeculativeState\":\n        bufs = self._spec_buffers[mode]\n        return self.SpeculativeState(\n            conv=self._spec_state_base[\"conv\"],\n            temporal=self._spec_state_base[\"temporal\"],\n            intermediate_ssm=bufs[\"intermediate_ssm\"],\n            intermediate_conv_window=bufs[\"intermediate_conv_window\"],\n        )\n\n    def set_active_spec_buffer(self, mode: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Switch the active intermediate spec buffer (D5 ↔ D7).\n\n        No-op when the pool is not in dynamic-spec split mode (legacy single\n        buffer or non-spec). Must be called before draft/verify forward so\n        consumers reading mamba_cache / mamba2_layer_cache pick up the right\n        buffer. CUDA-graph captures must do the same — the captured kernel\n        bakes in the tensor pointer, so D5 graph ↔ D5 buffer, D7 graph ↔ D7\n        buffer.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not self._dynamic_spec_split:\n            return\n        if mode == self._active_spec_buffer:\n            return\n        if mode not in self._spec_buffers:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Unknown spec buffer mode {mode!r}; expected one of \"\n                f\"{sorted(self._spec_buffers)}\"\n            )\n        self._active_spec_buffer = mode\n        self.mamba_cache = self._build_speculative_state(mode)\n\n    def get_speculative_mamba2_params_all_layers(self) -> SpeculativeState:\n        assert isinstance(self.mamba_cache, self.SpeculativeState)\n        return self.mamba_cache\n\n    def mamba2_layer_cache(self, layer_id: int):\n        return self.mamba_cache.at_layer_idx(layer_id)"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "    def handle_max_mamba_cache(self: ModelRunner, total_rest_memory):\n        config = self.mambaish_config\n        server_args = self.server_args\n        assert config is not None\n\n        # reserve the memory for the intermediate mamba states used for spec dec\n        if not self.spec_algorithm.is_none():\n            assert server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens is not None\n            assert server_args.max_running_requests is not None\n\n            max_running_requests = server_args.max_running_requests // (\n                self.dp_size if server_args.enable_dp_attention else 1\n            )\n            mamba_state_intermediate_size = (\n                config.mamba2_cache_params.mamba_cache_per_req\n                * max_running_requests\n                * server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens\n            )\n            total_rest_memory = total_rest_memory - (\n                mamba_state_intermediate_size / (1 << 30)\n            )",
  "new_string": "    def handle_max_mamba_cache(self: ModelRunner, total_rest_memory):\n        config = self.mambaish_config\n        server_args = self.server_args\n        assert config is not None\n\n        # reserve the memory for the intermediate mamba states used for spec dec\n        if not self.spec_algorithm.is_none():\n            assert server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens is not None\n            assert server_args.max_running_requests is not None\n\n            max_running_requests = server_args.max_running_requests // (\n                self.dp_size if server_args.enable_dp_attention else 1\n            )\n            d5_dtn, d7_dtn, d7_max_bs = self._get_dynamic_spec_split_dims()\n            if d5_dtn is not None:\n                # Dynamic-spec split: D5 buffer sized at (max_running×d5_dtn),\n                # D7 buffer sized at (d7_max_bs×d7_dtn). Far smaller than the\n                # single-buffer footprint (max_running × max(d5,d7)) when D7's\n                # max_bs is bounded by the hysteresis exit threshold.\n                intermediate_units = (\n                    max_running_requests * d5_dtn + d7_max_bs * d7_dtn\n                )\n            else:\n                intermediate_units = (\n                    max_running_requests * server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens\n                )\n            mamba_state_intermediate_size = (\n                config.mamba2_cache_params.mamba_cache_per_req * intermediate_units\n            )\n            total_rest_memory = total_rest_memory - (\n                mamba_state_intermediate_size / (1 << 30)\n            )"
}
```

```json
  "old_string": "    def handle_max_mamba_cache(self: ModelRunner, total_rest_memory):",
  "new_string": "    def _get_dynamic_spec_split_dims(self):\n        \"\"\"Resolve (d5_dtn, d7_dtn, d7_max_bs) for split-buffer mode, or\n        (None, None, None) when dynamic spec is disabled.\n\n        Pulls from EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE env config; only active when the spec\n        algorithm is EAGLE-family. Used by both the memory reservation in\n        handle_max_mamba_cache and the pool constructor in init_memory_pool\n        so the reserved budget matches the actual allocation.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.spec_algorithm.is_none() or not self.spec_algorithm.is_eagle():\n            return None, None, None\n        try:\n            from sglang.srt.speculative.spec_mode import (\n                load_config as _load_spec_cfg,\n            )\n        except ImportError:\n            return None, None, None\n        cfg = _load_spec_cfg()\n        if not cfg.enabled:\n            return None, None, None\n        # D7 active running bs is bounded by hysteresis (bs ∈ [0, leave_d7_bs-1]);\n        # buffer needs `leave_d7_bs+1` rows to index up to `leave_d7_bs-1`.\n        return cfg.d5_dtn, cfg.d7_dtn, cfg.leave_d7_bs\n\n    def handle_max_mamba_cache(self: ModelRunner, total_rest_memory):"
}
```

```
685	    def _apply_spec_config(self, mode: SpecMode) -> None:
686	        """Switch worker spec config (topk / steps / dtn) and active draft graph.
687	
688	        No-op if dynamic mode disabled or mode is the same as last applied. The
689	        draft attn_backend is shared across modes (sized to ``_max_spec_steps``
690	        at init); only the cuda-graph runner buffers differ.
691	        """
692	        cfg = self.spec_mode_config
693	        if not cfg.enabled:
694	            return
695	        if mode == SpecMode.MARS_D5:
696	            new_topk, new_steps, new_dtn = cfg.d5_topk, cfg.d5_steps, cfg.d5_dtn
697	            new_runner = self.cuda_graph_runner_d5
698	            new_theta = cfg.effective_d5_theta()
699	        elif mode == SpecMode.MARS_D7:
700	            new_topk, new_steps, new_dtn = cfg.d7_topk, cfg.d7_steps, cfg.d7_dtn
701	            new_runner = self.cuda_graph_runner_d7
702	            new_theta = cfg.effective_d7_theta()
703	        else:
704	            return  # NO_SPEC handled separately
705	
706	        # Steady-state fast path: D5 -> D5 / D7 -> D7 are the vast majority of
707	        # decode steps once the bs settles. _applied_spec_mode is None on the
708	        # very first call (forces full re-bind so cuda_graph_runner is correct
709	        # even if self.* defaults already match the requested mode).
710	        if self._applied_spec_mode == mode:
711	            return
712	
713	        self.topk = new_topk
714	        self.speculative_num_steps = new_steps
715	        self.speculative_num_draft_tokens = new_dtn
716	        self.server_args.speculative_eagle_topk = new_topk
717	        self.server_args.speculative_num_steps = new_steps
718	        self.server_args.speculative_num_draft_tokens = new_dtn
719	        self.cuda_graph_runner = new_runner
720	
721	        # Per-mode MARS theta. eagle_info reads from EagleVerifyInput.mars_theta
722	        # (plumbed via draft() at construction time) so we just stash the value
723	        # on self here. D7 (typically bs=1, S1) can run a more aggressive theta
724	        # than D5 without affecting D5 quality. -1 disables MARS for that mode.
725	        self._active_mars_theta = float(new_theta)
726	        self._applied_spec_mode = mode
727

729	        """Run target_worker direct decode and update draft KV immediately."""

731	        if batch.output_ids is None or batch.output_ids.numel() != bs:

734	            )
735	            logger.warning(
```

```
620	        def _capture_d5_verify_stream(stream_idx: Optional[int] = None):
621	            """Capture EAGLE D5 TARGET_VERIFY graphs (dtn=5, smaller than max=7).
622	
623	            The default _capture_one_stream already captures TARGET_VERIFY at
624	            dtn=max=7 (D7 mode). This adds a second TARGET_VERIFY capture at
625	            dtn=5 (D5 mode) so dynamic mode can switch dtn without falling off
626	            cuda graph. Both graphs share the same input buffers (sized to max).
627	            """
628	            capture_range = (
629	                tqdm.tqdm(list(reversed(self.capture_bs)), desc="Capturing D5 verify graphs")
630	                if get_tensor_model_parallel_rank() == 0
631	                else reversed(self.capture_bs)
632	            )
633	            d5_dtn = self.eagle_d5_dtn
634	            for bs in capture_range:
635	                with patch_model(
636	                    self.model_runner.model,
637	                    self.model_runner.server_args.enable_torch_compile and bs in self.compile_bs,
638	                    num_tokens=bs * d5_dtn,
639	                    tp_group=self.model_runner.tp_group,
640	                ) as forward:
641	                    graph, output_buffers = self.capture_one_batch_size(
642	                        bs, forward, stream_idx,
643	                        override_forward_mode=ForwardMode.TARGET_VERIFY,
644	                        override_num_tokens_per_bs=d5_dtn,
645	                    )
646	                    key = f"d5_{bs}" if stream_idx is None else f"{stream_idx}_d5_{bs}"
647	                    self.graphs[key] = graph
648	                    self.output_buffers[key] = output_buffers
649	
650	        # Trigger CUDA graph capture for specific shapes.
651	        # Capture the large shapes first so that the smaller shapes
652	        # can reuse the memory pool allocated for the large shapes.
653	        with freeze_gc(self.model_runner.server_args.enable_cudagraph_gc):
654	            if not self.enable_pdmux:
655	                with graph_capture() as graph_capture_context, profile_context as prof:
656	                    self.stream = graph_capture_context.stream

658	                    if getattr(self, 'has_eagle_d5_verify_graphs', False):
```

```
608	                    num_tokens=bs,  # 1 token per request
609	                    tp_group=self.model_runner.tp_group,
610	                ) as forward:

612	                        bs, forward, stream_idx,
613	                        override_forward_mode=ForwardMode.DECODE,
614	                        override_num_tokens_per_bs=1,
615	                    )
616	                    key = f"decode_{bs}" if stream_idx is None else f"{stream_idx}_decode_{bs}"
617	                    self.graphs[key] = graph
618	                    self.output_buffers[key] = output_buffers
```

| `intermediate_ssm` | **19.08 GB** | **14.20 GB** (D5=12.14 + D7=2.06) | **−4.88 GB** |
| `max_total_num_tokens` | 3,966,050 | **4,648,034** | **+17.2%** |
| 行数×dtn | 37 × 11 = 407 | 37×7 + 4×11 = 303 | -25.6% |

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
# =============================================================
# mini_bench.sh — 快速速度验证
#
# 用法:
#   bash bench/mini_bench.sh [API_BASE] [N_S1] [N_S8] [N_SMAX]
#
# 默认值:
#   API_BASE = http://127.0.0.1:30000
#   N_S1=8  N_S8=24  N_SMAX=64
#
# S8=24 覆盖三个满批，能体现并发压力；Smax=64 覆盖长尾。
# 数据集嵌套关系: S1(8) ⊆ S8(24) ⊆ Smax(64)，每档样本唯一。
# 本脚本取各档前 N 条，保留与全量相同的长尾特性。
# =============================================================

API_BASE="${1:-http://127.0.0.1:30000}"
N_S1="${2:-8}"
N_S8="${3:-24}"
N_SMAX="${4:-64}"

BENCH_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
DATA_DIR="${BENCH_DIR}/data"
TOOLKIT_BENCH="${BENCH_DIR}/../toolkit/bench_serving.sh"

echo "=========================================================="
echo "  mini_bench  S1=${N_S1}  S8=${N_S8}  Smax=${N_SMAX}"
echo "  API: ${API_BASE}"
echo "=========================================================="

# 分层抽样到 /tmp（按 prompt_tokens 分布均匀取样，避免前段全是短请求）
S1_MINI="/tmp/mini_s1.jsonl"
S8_MINI="/tmp/mini_s8.jsonl"
SMAX_MINI="/tmp/mini_smax.jsonl"

python3 - <<PY
import json, random

SEED = 42
```

## 总结

### 调试确定的根因
`MambaPool.intermediate_ssm_state_cache` 在 dynamic spec 模式下被按 `max(d5_dtn, d7_dtn) × max_running_requests` 一次性分配，从 `total_rest_memory` 直接扣，把 KV pool 砍掉 ~5 GB（max_running=36 现状），造成 prefill admission 在 KV/mamba 池接近饱和时提前停。

实测在 `max_running=36, D5_DTN=7, D7_DTN=11`：
- 改前：`intermediate_ssm = 19.08 GB`，`max_total_num_tokens = 3,966,050`
- 改后：`intermediate_ssm = 14.20 GB`（D5=12.14 + D7=2.06），`max_total_num_tokens = 4,648,034` (**+17.2%**)

### 方案 2 实现
4 文件 240 行：

1. **`mem_cache/memory_pool.py`** — `MambaPool` 增加 `spec_d5_dtn / spec_d7_dtn / spec_d7_max_bs` 三参数；提供 D5（`max_running+1` 行 × `d5_dtn`）和 D7（`leave_d7_bs+1` 行 × `d7_dtn`）两块 buffer，`mamba_cache` 切换时 rebuild 同一份 conv/temporal + 不同 intermediate；新增 `set_active_spec_buffer("d5"|"d7")`。`HybridReqToTokenPool` / `MiniCPMHybridReqToTokenPool` 透传新参数。
2. **`model_runner_kv_cache_mixin.py`** — `_get_dynamic_spec_split_dims()` 从 `EAGLE_DYNAMIC_MODE` 解析配置；`handle_max_mamba_cache` 改用 `max_running × d5_dtn + d7_max_bs × d7_dtn` 估预留；池构造透传新参数。
3. **`speculative/eagle_worker.py`** — 加 `_set_mamba_spec_buffer(mode)` helper；`_apply_spec_config(MARS_D5/D7)` 切换；draft cuda graph capture D5/D7 各自切。
4. **`model_executor/cuda_graph_runner.py`** — target verify graph capture D7（默认）/ D5 graph 各自切，runtime default 留 D5。

### 测试结果
| 测试 | 结果 |
|---|---|
| Init sizing 实测 | KV pool +17.2%，intermediate_ssm −4.88 GB |
| 3 条 chat 冒烟 | 全 deterministic、说人话 |
| 4 mode transition | D5 → D7 → NO_SPEC → D5 → D7 全部 PASS，无 illegal memory / retract |
| mini_bench S1/S8/Smax | S1 持平、S8 +0.65%（噪声）、Smax **−3.75%**（KV 池增大→admission 更宽松） |

未提交。是否要提交？或者你想先把 `max_running_requests` 抬到 64 再实测一遍？

tool_use Bash
```json
{
  "command": "git add demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/mem_cache/memory_pool.py demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/model_executor/cuda_graph_runner.py demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/model_executor/model_runner_kv_cache_mixin.py demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/speculative/eagle_worker.py\ngit commit -m \"$(cat <<'EOF'\nspec: split D5/D7 mamba intermediate buffer to free KV pool\n\nDynamic spec mode was sizing the per-request mamba intermediate state\ncache at max(d5_dtn, d7_dtn) × max_running_requests rows × dtn — the\nsingle buffer feeds both D5 (active at most batch sizes) and D7 (only\nactive at bs ≤ leave_d7_bs-1, sized at cap_max_bs=1 in the cuda graph).\nThat sizing is needlessly conservative: D7's row dimension never needs\nmax_running_requests, only leave_d7_bs+1.\n\nSplit the cache into two buffers:\n  D5: (num_layers, max_running+1, d5_dtn, ...)  — full-batch\n  D7: (num_layers, leave_d7_bs+1, d7_dtn, ...)  — bs-capped\nMambaPool.set_active_spec_buffer(\"d5\"|\"d7\") swaps which buffer the\nSpeculativeState exposes; consumers (hybrid_linear_attn_backend,\nmamba.py) read through mamba_cache without touching the buffers\ndirectly. EAGLE worker swaps in _apply_spec_config; cuda graph capture\nswaps before D5 vs D7 verify capture so the graph bakes in the right\npointer.\n\nFootprint impact at max_running=36, D5_DTN=7, D7_DTN=11:\n  intermediate_ssm: 19.08 GB → 14.20 GB (D5=12.14 + D7=2.06, −25.6%)\n  max_total_num_tokens: 3.97M → 4.65M (+17.2%)\nAt max_running=64 the savings grow to ~10 GB (rows×dtn 715 → 499).\n\nmini_bench (max_running=36, dynamic D5/D7=7/11):\n  S1=8:    134.66s → 134.66s  (=)\n  S8=24:   233.57s → 235.08s  (+0.65%, noise)\n  Smax=24: 236.89s → 228.01s  (−3.75%, KV pool unblocks admission)\n4-mode transition (D5↔D7↔NO_SPEC) PASS on smoke + bench.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\"\ngit log --oneline -3",
  "description": "commit split-D5D7 改动"
}
```
